# Foldie Project — Black Lacquer Superlight Build — Parts Research (full detail)

Round 2 (24 Oct 2026): eight parallel research passes, no 10-brand cap. Every row has a weight (g) and a price (S$). A value shown as `est.` is an estimate; the **Basis** column says how it was worked out (`page` read on a vendor page, `forum` owner/forum measurement, `listing` marketplace ad, `snippet` search snippet only, `est.` estimate). Prices are vendor/list prices converted at fixed rates (US$1=S$1.30, £1=S$1.70, €1=S$1.50), before shipping, GST and duty. Carousell, Facebook Marketplace, Shopee and Lazada could not be read directly (bot checks, not bypassed), so marketplace prices are from search snippets or are estimates.

Compact list: `C_Line_Parts_List.md`.


---

## Frame: stem, fork, rear triangle, headset, hinge hardware


Group: stems, forks, rear triangles (and fork+triangle sets), headsets, frame hinge hardware (not clamps). Extends `parts_A_stem_fork_triangle.md`. FX fixed per brief (USD 1.30, GBP 1.70, EUR 1.50, CNY 0.18; CAD is not in the brief, so 1 CAD = 0.95 is my own assumed rate, marked est.). SG = price shown in S$ by a Singapore store.

Baselines (stock steel, forum list by chagzuki on YACF 2012, "mix of official weights and my own"): stem S 866 / M 806 / P 602 g; fork 565 g (Ti 350 g in same list); rear triangle 755 g (Ti 468 g); headset steel 165 g, aluminium 120 g. Genuine Ti triangle 461 g (SJS).

**Warning on Fantastic4Toys SG weights:** the `grams` field in the F4 product data is a shipping placeholder (stem 1000 g, headsets 100/150/300 g, H&H set 4000 g, Silverock 2000 g). I did not use those. Use the weights in the page text or the estimates here.

Cone/expander vs wedge: Brompton's classic quill stem is clamped inside the head tube by a pull-bolt and a cone-type expander; the newer "wedge" arrangement appears on 2018+ designs. No seller below publishes this for its stem except where stated, so the per-row note says "unconfirmed" and tells you to ask for the expander version on a pre-C / early C bike.

Notes
- No stem page found for H&H, Silverock, Litepro, Aceoffix, Bjorn Cycles, thx4ride, FeatherWorx, Ridea, Titanium-DX, Tibike, J&L, London Craftwork, Kinetics, Twisted Wheels, CarbonDry, ACE, Sinclair. Brompton OEM Ti stem is T/P Line only.
- Several of these are the same Chinese factory (Comeplay, Hexati, TiBicycles, probably EasyTi, Ti Atom, unbranded), so the "estimated" weights are a reasonable bracket (440-560 g).

- Stock steel fork 565 g; Ti saves ~280-300 g.

### Count and gaps


- Stem 12 rows (11 products + 1 reference); fork 14 rows; rear triangle 12 + 7 set rows; headset 16 rows; hinge hardware 4 rows. Total about 65.
- Estimated cells (`est.`): stem weights 7, stem prices 2; fork weights 3 (H&H, EasyTi, Titanium-DX) plus price estimates 3; triangle weights 6, prices 4; headset weights 11; hinge 3 (weight 2, price 1).
- Could not read: Carousell (403), bromptuning.com / FeatherWorx (certificate error), Titanium-DX eBay (403), eBay Ti pivot spindle 273124616340 (403), Taobao/Shopee/Lazada. No product pages found for thx4ride, Bjorn Cycles, Aceoffix, FeatherWorx, Litepro, Silverock stem, Tibike, J&L, Twisted Wheels, CarbonDry, ACE, Sinclair for stem/fork/triangle. These are searched but not listed.
- Fork offset and brake-hole diameter were not published by any seller; steerer length only for Silverock and one TiBicycles page.
- Cone/expander vs wedge not published by Comeplay, UXN, Hexati, EasyTi, Ti Atom, TIBROMTACK, B-Spokes; only TiBicycles states wedge.

## Wheels, hubs, rims, spokes


Researched 24 Oct 2026. FX fixed: 1 USD = 1.30, 1 GBP = 1.70, 1 EUR = 1.50, 1 CNY = 0.18, 1 HKD = 0.17, 1 TWD = 0.04. S$ rounded to nearest dollar. "SG" = shown in S$ by a Singapore store (Fantastic4Toys en-sg storefront, BZ Sport, Brompton Kitchen, B-Spokes, Wheel Angel SG). Weights are vendor claims unless Basis says otherwise. Round-1 file parts_B section 1 is reused and extended.

**Read this first: the HG problem.** Your Neutron B7 is a Shimano HG-spline cassette. Only wheels whose rear hub has a true Shimano HG (HyperGlide) driver take it directly. Verified HG: Light Bicycle BR01 (Shimano 7-speed freehub), BZ BN16 / Hubsmith RS01R/RS42R / Onyx (HyperGlide driver), Wheel Angel Onyx BMX Pro HG. BUT BZ pages limit standard Shimano cassettes to 4-5 speed (cog spacing/dish on a 112 mm axle); only H&H/Hex/MiniMODs unibody cassettes go to 6-7. Only Light Bicycle (Shimano 7-speed freehub) and the Hubsmith Bumbee (Shimano 7SP-CE) say 7-speed Shimano. SMC, Suncord, Union Jack, Ridea, Extralite, Silverock use ratchet / threaded / unibody bodies, not stated HG. Check with the seller before buying anything marked "HG not stated".

Column "Weight (g)": set/pair/each is stated in the cell.

### Count and gaps


Rows: Complete wheelsets 39 (inc. Dcch and Twisted Wheels), front wheels 7, rear wheels 4, front hubs 9, rear hubs 12, carbon rims 7, alloy rims 8, spokes/nipples 10 (DT Aerolite row is empty on purpose: a placeholder, not counted as data).

Estimated cells (labelled est.): weight in about 24 rows (BZ Onyx sets, Bumbee A349, Silverock set/hubs, Suncord 5-spoke, Carboncian 5-spoke, Ridea price, Kuosac A22C and 4-7 hubs, Dcch, Twisted Wheels weight+price, SMC rear wheels, B-Spokes rear wheel, SP-B, Jtek hub, Brompton stock front, H&H hubs, White Industries, spokes). Prices est.: Ridea carbon, Twisted Wheels, Jtek hub, stock front hub, Sapim Polyax, Jtek spokes.

Not accessible / not found:
- Bjorn Cycles: its site and the Twisted Wheels page list only composites (Glagol carbon seatpost GBP 309, Rool bars, Sedlo saddle). No wheels or hubs exist. Carbon seatposts are excluded for you anyway.
- CarbonDry (Japan): complete carbon bikes only (3.9-5.9 kg), no separately sold wheels, hubs or rims found; site carbondryjapan.com does not exist as typed (CDJ site lists bearing kits).
- Twisted Wheels: sells custom builds via Linktree only; no published price or wheel weight. Row is an estimate.
- Dcch wheelset: only the Carousell S$500 ad from the repo; Carousell is behind a bot check, not bypassed. B-Spokes lists Dcch drivetrain/headsets only.
- Firecrawl was blocked (VPN/proxy key requirement), so all lookups used WebSearch/WebFetch. Prices on speedminicycle.com are USD and may differ from the SG dealer.
- DT Aerolite, Sapim CX-Ray 349 lengths and set prices: no 349-specific source found. Chris King, Novatec, DT Swiss 74/112 hubs: do not exist as products.
- Wheel Angel SG store price only read for the Extralite set (S$2,180); other Wheel Angel sets use the US store (USD converted).
- Extralite fit to Neutron B7: no source states HG or 7-speed for the SP-2B/SL-2B. Treat as unfit until Extralite confirms.

## Cranks and bottom brackets


Rates used: 1 USD = 1.30, 1 GBP = 1.70, 1 EUR = 1.50 S$. SG = price shown in S$ by a Singapore seller. Extends round-1 file parts_C_drivetrain.md section 6.


### Fit summary (read first)


- **C Line shell:** Brompton's stock bottom bracket is a 68 mm wide BSA-threaded shell (1.37 x 24 TPI) taking a JIS 119 mm square-taper cartridge with plastic cups (Clever Cycles, SJS, Token listing: "BSA threaded frame shell, 68 mm wide"). So the thread is the standard BSA type. I did NOT find a Brompton or forum statement specific to the C Line shell beyond this; round 1 repo notes say the C Line shell must be faced for 30 mm external BBs.
- **Three crank families fit this shell:**
  1. **Stock-style square taper JIS 119 mm** (Brompton, H&H "square taper", TPW square taper, Union Jack Poker): drop in with the stock BB or a lighter 119 mm Ti BB.
  2. **30 mm spindle, external BSA30 cups** (eeWings, H&H alloy, TPW hollow, Superlight carbon, White Ind. R30, Hope Evo, Praxis M30, Extralite QRC-3S, Union Jack BSA30, Kuosac): needs a BSA30 BB; SJS advises facing the shell for alignment.
  3. **24 mm or DUB 28.99 mm road spindles** (Elilee X310/X260, Aceoffix DUB, Neutron, Rotor, Shimano, SRAM, FSA, Chris King BSA24 BB): sold by Singapore sellers as "for Brompton A/C/P/T" (Elilee) but BB, chainline and frame clearance are the seller's claim only.
- **Chainline:** Elilee lists 44.5 mm, Ridea Pneuma 48 mm, Praxis Cadet 49-52 mm. Owner's 112 mm rear with a 7-speed Brompton-spaced cassette needs a measured chainline; none of the listings state a Brompton C Line figure. Kuosac arms can catch the rear triangle when folded (SJS: add ~3 mm BB spacer).
- **Chainring:** Brompton spider = 130 BCD, 5-arm. Road/direct-mount arms (Elilee, eeWings 8-bolt, Neutron, Extralite 110 BCD, Union Jack BB30) need a matching spider or direct-mount ring. See round-1 section 5 for rings.

### Notes


- **Eilileee spelling:** All sources use "Elilee" (elilee.com). Brand sells X310 (310-340 g) and X260 (255 g). One WW forum user warned a dealer might substitute an older X320 as an X310: ask for proof of model.
- **eeWings:** Standard eeWings is a 30 mm-spindle crank, no ring or spider included. Only SG resellers describe a Brompton version; Cane Creek does not list one. A BSA30 BB must be bought separately (SG seller says so).
- **TPW price inconsistency:** F4T shows hollow crank US$665 alone and "from US$1,020" for crank + Ti BSA30 BB set, but the set page shows US$1,200. Used US$1,200 for the set row.
- **Prices for the same Elilee X310 on Carousell SG are exclusive of chainring and installation.**
- Lightest realistic Brompton-oriented complete options found: Elilee X260 255 g arms (S$965), eeWings 398 g (S$1,400), Aceoffix 385 g with ring (S$286), Elilee X310 310 g (S$450-550), Extralite QRC-3S 348 g (S$1,261), LitzMo 366 g (S$1,033 with BB).


### Count and gaps


- **Cranksets/arms rows:** 43. **Bottom bracket rows:** 13.
- **Estimated cells (marked est.):** crank weights: Cane Creek Aegean, TPW Ti square taper, TPW set, Kuosac set, Union Jack BSA30 and BCD110, Ridea ES, Rpantahi, thx4ride, ROLLCii, generic polished (11), plus Cane Creek spider; BB weights: Token, Philwood, ROLLCii, Cane Creek, White Industries, Shimano (6) and one BB price (Shimano). Total about 19 estimated cells.
- **Not found (no row):** Truvativ, Lightning (Lightning Cycle Dynamics, recumbent carbon cranks), Bjorn Cycles (no product page located), Kuosac chainring weights, Union Jack Poker weight (re-checked: conflicting), H&H Ti BB thread width. Bjorn, Lightning and Truvativ left out because no Brompton listing exists and I would have had to invent a product; Truvativ is covered by SRAM Apex DUB as the closest.
- **Not accessible:** Carousell SG listing pages (HTTP 403 to fetch; only search-engine snippets of prices), the Carousell eeWings and Elilee search pages, eBay item pages (403), underdogscycling.cc (DNS fail), Brompton Kitchen collection page (404), firecrawl (proxy block). Praxis "Turn" cranks not priced (US$299 per Bikerumor/BikeRadar snippet, claimed ~790 g with BB).
- **Facebook Marketplace, Taobao and AliExpress listings** were not directly reachable; the only AliExpress price is a 2023 WeightWeenies forum quote.


---

## Chainrings


Fixed rates used: USD 1.30, GBP 1.70, EUR 1.50, CAD not in brief (CAD not converted, see Notes). Weights are the single ring unless stated. Brompton spider crank = 130 BCD, 5 arm. Rings of 130 BCD fit it mechanically; very few vendors state "Brompton fit". Owner target: 38-46T for a 7-speed 11-30T cassette. 130 BCD limits the smallest ring to about 38T (Cycling UK forum, snippet); 40T and 44T are the usual low options. Fantastic4Toys is listed as "F4T" (ships to SG; price shown in USD, no SG pricing seen). Sources read this session on 24 Oct 2026 unless stated.

### Notes

- Best fit for the owner's 38-46T target on a standard Brompton 130 spider: Pass Quest 40-46T (54-76 g, S$65), Wolf Tooth 38-44T (52-79 g, S$110), absoluteBLACK round 38/42T (49/64 g), H&H 40T/44T with built-in guard (60/68 g, S$106). Carbon: Lightworks 42-46T (42-49 g, S$194), Carbon-Ti 39T (~45 g). Suncord, Digirit and FSA have no usable 38-44T data or fit.
- Chain: all 130 BCD narrow-wide and carbon rings above are 1/2" x 11/128" or 3/32" pitch; for a 7-speed cassette with a 1x ring a normal 7/8-speed chain works, SJS notes 130 BCD non-Brompton rings use 3/32 chain. Check each ring against the chain (Pass Quest, Wolf Tooth, Garbaruk: 9-12 sp). A 40-44T ring needs about 2-4 fewer links.
- Several Fantastic4Toys prices are USD with SG shipping at checkout; Fantastic4Toys does not publish a SG-dollar price in the pages read. Brompton SG OEM price S$70 is the only SG-dollar retail price found.
- Titanium: no real solid-titanium chainring exists in the Brompton 130 BCD market; "titanium" in listings is plating or coating (Litepro, Bespoke Ti Gold, H&H colours) except an eBay H&H duo flagged "titanium" (unverified). Ti is used for bolts only.
- CAD not in the brief rates; the Garbaruk Sweet Pete's row assumes CAD 1 = S$1.00 and is an estimate.


### Count and gaps

- Rows: Part 1 = 36, Part 2 = 9, Part 3 = 4 (49 total, counting each weight/size as one row or grouped as shown).
- Estimates (`est.` in weight or price cell): about 21 cells (H&H 48T weight, Garbaruk CAD price and aero price, absoluteBLACK round price, Ridea R5ST weight, Alugear weight, Bespoke Black and Ti Gold weights, Suncord 44T weight, Digirit weight, Sugino weight, SRAM weight, Kuosac weight, Rotor price, Praxis weight, generic price, Pass Quest hollow weight and price, thx4ride row, FSA weight, Pass Quest spider weight, Booyoung weight, bolts weight).
- Could not access: Garbaruk page (redirect, weights only from snippet), Weight Weenies Extralite thread (403), Carousell thx4ride/ Litepro pages (403, snippet used), eBay listings (403), Sugino and absoluteBLACK round pages (404/DNS), Ridea PDF weights, Brompton SG and B-Spokes/BZ Sport/16B/Cyclopes product pages (not fetched), Shopee/Lazada (not opened).
- Brands asked for but with no 130 BCD Brompton ring found: Aceoffix, TPW (only aluminium spider for T Line), MiniMODs, Bjorn Cycles, Deda, Miche, Rotor (52T only), Praxis (sets only), Extralite (110 BCD only), thx4ride (no ring), Cane Creek (spider only, 110 BCD). Pass Quest exists, 130 BCD, 40-60T.

## Shifters, derailleurs, cassettes, chains


Context: Brompton C Line rebuild, own external 7-speed (Shimano HG freehub, Neutron B7 11-13-15-18-21-23-30T cassette). FX fixed: 1 USD 1.30, 1 GBP 1.70, 1 EUR 1.50, 1 AUD 0.85 S$. Prices exclude shipping/GST/duty. `~N est.` = estimate (see Basis). Round-1 rows kept and extended; new rows added. Weight basis: each unless "set/pair" is stated.

SG price finding: Fantastic4Toys en-sg shows the H&H C Line set at S$347 (page region selector = Singapore, currency symbol only "$"; US page US$266 x 1.30 = S$346). So F4T Singapore prices look like USD x 1.30; I use that for F4T rows (marked "SG?" where only the US page was read). Shipping to Singapore is "most countries" on page, not confirmed.

Carousell SG, Shopee SG, Lazada SG, B-Spokes, Brompton Kitchen SG, BZ Sport, 16B Cycle, Cyclopes Co pages were not readable or had no prices (Carousell gives Cloudflare checks; not bypassed). Singapore-only prices therefore exist only for the two ZTTO jockey wheel listings and the thx4ride Carousell leads.

Notes
- Pull ratio: shifter and derailleur must match (Shimano road 8/9/10 differ from MTB; Eagle, microSHIFT Advent X and SRAM have own pulls). With 7-speed cogs the safe matches are 7-speed Shimano/Sunrace/microSHIFT/L-Twoo triggers or the Brompton-specific thumb shifters.
- Eagle implication: 12-speed Eagle shifters (mechanical or AXS) need a 12-speed Eagle derailleur, 12-speed chain and a 12-cog cassette (10-50T). They cannot index the 7-cog Neutron B7. Lightest option is the AXS controller (68 g) or XX SL pod (48 g), but the whole chain-of-parts goes 12-speed (derailleur ~350 g est., not for 16" bikes). Not recommended.
- Deore M4100 195 g and Altus 223 g look like pair/packaging numbers; real single triggers are about 110-130 g.

### Count and gaps

- Shifters: 40 rows (about 26 with real weight; 14 est. weights and 17 est. prices). Derailleurs/tensioners: 24 rows, 18 est. cells. Pulleys: 6 rows, 3 est. Cassettes: 11 rows, 8 est. cells. Chains: 16 rows, ~14 est. cells.
- Not accessed: Carousell, Shopee, Lazada, Brompton Kitchen SG, B-Spokes pages (no public prices); BromptonTalk, WeightWeenies had no scale posts for these sets. No scale weight found for H&H, MiniMODs, ACE, Suncord, thx4ride sets.
- Not found: Titanium pulley wheels with a price; Neutron/Rotor/Hubsmith derailleurs; Sensah 7-speed shifter.

## Brakes, levers, hinge clamps, pads, cables


Rates: 1 USD = 1.30, 1 GBP = 1.70, 1 EUR = 1.50, 1 JPY = 0.0087 S$. "SG" = price shown in S$ by a Singapore shop. Rows marked `est.` have an estimated cell, explained in Basis. Extends round 1 file parts_D_brakes_cockpit.md sections 1-2.

Key fit facts: Brompton 2018+ caliper = short-pull (road style), 54-68 mm drop. Every flat-bar lever below is tagged SHORT or LONG pull. LONG-pull (V-brake) levers under-power Brompton-type calipers. Rim brake calipers are priced per brake unless "pair/set" is stated.

- eeBrake family counted: eeBrake Brompton, eeBrake G4 Brompton Edition, G4 Brompton mirror image, EL Platino (limited), plus the non-Brompton G4. Weights: 169.0 g pair (G4-type, 85.3/83.7) vs 206.8 g pair (original Brompton eeBrake, 105.4/101.4): sellers differ, G4 is the lighter newer one. Both are from sellers' pages without pads.
- Fit with mudguards/rack: bluelug, Grumpy, EL Platino listing all say NOT compatible with mudguards or racks. Clever Cycles' copy says the opposite (helps offset rack/light weight) but is the odd one out; treat as incompatible.
- Not found with price: Tektro RC-483 extra deep drop 55-73 mm (Condor, no price visible), Dia-Compe BRS101/BRS100 (S$48 SG; reach not stated), Ritchey, Magura, SRAM, Kuosac, Aceoffix, H&H, TRP calipers: no Brompton-reach rim caliper found.

### Count and gaps


Rows per part: calipers 15; levers 24; hinge clamps 10; pads 7; cables 9. Total 65.

Estimated cells (`est.`): calipers 3 (Curbside S$, Clever weight, Dia-Compe weight, Shimano price = counted 4); levers about 13 (thx4ride x3 weights, Aceoffix price, Shimano Claris/Tiagra weights and Tiagra price, used Extralite weight, Promax unit, TRP weight, Velo Orange weight, Cane Creek weight); hinge clamps 6 (OEM price, Kuosac weight, Brompfication plates weight, Carousell G-line weight, thx4ride weight and price); pads 5 weights; cables 8 (Jagwire x2 weights, Nokon, Alligator, Gore, thx4ride). About 36 estimated cells in all, all labelled.

Could not access: Carousell SG search pages (403, only search snippets); R2-Bike lever pages (403, no prices); eBay.de listings (403); Facebook Marketplace; Fantastic4Toys SG, B-Spokes, BZ Sport, 16B Cycle, Cyclopes Co, Shopee, Lazada (no listings reachable); Temple Cycles SG page (404); firecrawl (blocked, VPN). No S$ prices for Extralite 3S, Swissstop, hinge clamps, or Brompton parts from an SG shop (only Cycle Project Store SG for Extralite 3, eeBrake G4, Paul, Dia-Compe, TRP). Searched but found nothing: Kuosac/Ritchey/Magura/SRAM levers, Aceoffix/Ridea/Bjorn hinge clamps, thx4ride hinge clamp, Swissstop "Green King".

## Cockpit, contact points, tyres, tubes, small parts


Research date: 24 Oct 2026. FX fixed per brief (1 USD = 1.30, 1 GBP = 1.70, 1 EUR = 1.50, 1 AUD = 0.85 S$; S$ rounded). Fantastic4Toys (F4T) and Clever Cycles prices are the US$ list prices from their Shopify catalogues read on 24 Oct 2026 (the F4T site fetched is the US$ storefront; the en-sg storefront was not reachable, so S$ = US$ x 1.30, before shipping/GST). Brompton Kitchen (BK) and B-Spokes (BS) prices are the Singapore S$ shelf prices from their Shopify catalogues read on 24 Oct 2026 (in-stock status not guaranteed; some items showed sold out).

Weight legend: weights marked `page` were read in the product description text of the vendor page (vendor claims, not scale-weighed). `~N est.` = estimate, reason in the Basis column. "pair/each/set" is stated in the cell. Brompton Kitchen / B-Spokes catalogue "grams" fields are shipping weights and were NOT used as product weights.

### Notes (cross-cutting)


- Carbon seatposts were left out on purpose (child seat on the post): Brompton Superlight Carbon (S$265 B-Spokes), H&H carbon (from S$200), ACE carbon (US$47-60), WOOdman Carbo GT2, Ridea carbon (S$278), Schmolke TLO, Bjorn Glagol (US$330, 31.6 not 31.8 per filters).
- Bjorn Cycles (Slovenia, composite parts, US$ prices, sold in the US by Fair Wheel Bikes): no Brompton-specific listing exists. Rows: saddles Sedlo, Probka, Kort, Divan, Setka; Skoba bottle cage; Rool bar (not a fit). Saddle rails are composite, so expect to need the Brompton/TPW rail adapter. No Singapore reseller found.
- Tyres: no 349 product found for Kenda, Maxxis, CST (only a 37-349 wire size in a size chart), Vittoria, Challenge, Michelin (tube only), Tioga, Vredestein. Primo Comet (349 per one retailer, 305 per another) and Tannus airless are the only extra brands with real data. Kenda Kwest is 16 x 1.5 (305), not a Brompton size.
- Weight conflicts: Schwalbe One 173 vs 175 (REI 250), Kojak folding 175/181/190 (Schwalbe page 295 is a 26" copy error), Contact Urban 205/220/265, Marathon Racer 230 folding vs 270-280 wire, Brompton Flexi-Mudguard 75 vs 180.
- Brompton Kitchen / B-Spokes catalogues also stock Ti Parts Workshop "Mini P" and ColourLab/other items not listed here; stock status was not checked row by row.
- Pedal-holder parts for MKS Ezy pedals (H&H S$25-35, TPW S$55-80, Union Jack US$10-30) are not listed as rows; ask if wanted.


### Count and gaps


- Rows per part: Handlebars 43, Grips 32, Bar ends 6, Seatposts 24, Saddles 41, Pedals 34, Tyres 33, Tubes 16, Mudguards 24, Racks 29, Easy wheels 25, Lights 26, Bottle cages 15, Bells 10, Ti bolts 36, Cable guides 10. Total 404.
- Estimated weight cells (`~N est.`): about 137 of 404. Concentrated in grips (19), lights (14), saddles (14), mudguards (13), bottle cages (9), racks (9), where vendors publish no weight. Reasons are in each Basis cell.
- Estimated price cells (not read on a vendor page): Selle Italia SLR Boost (GBP 120 est.), Primo Comet (US$25 est.), Tannus Aither (GBP 62 est. = S$105), MKS Sylvan Road Next (US$100 est.), unbranded SG Ti seatpost (mid of S$95-170 asks), Brooks C13 (mid of US$198-220), SMP Well (mid of EUR 90-112), Litepro A61 (Carousell ask).
- Could not access: Carousell SG and Shopee/Lazada (no reliable public reads), Facebook Marketplace (login), the F4T en-sg storefront (prices therefore US$ x 1.30), 16B Cycle, Cyclopes Co, BZ Sport accessory pages (cockpit parts not stocked there per round 1), bjorncycles.com sub-pages (404/Cloudflare-style blocks), Firecrawl (blocked on a proxy IP), SJS and other UK pages beyond what search snippets showed.
- "Price S$" for Brompton Kitchen and B-Spokes rows is the Singapore shelf price; for F4T and Clever Cycles it is the US$ list price x 1.30 before shipping and GST.

## Bjorn Cycles (Slovenian carbon parts maker)



### Who Bjorn Cycles is (identification)


- Searched: "Bjorn Cycles Brompton", "bjorncycles Brompton parts", "Bjorn Cycles Singapore bicycle shop", "Bjorn Cycles Carousell/Shopee/Instagram/eBay folding bike", "Bjorn Cycles Brompton titanium parts Singapore". No Brompton shop, Singapore shop, Carousell, Shopee or eBay store called Bjorn Cycles turned up in any of them.
- **The business that exists under this name is Bjorn Cycles / Bjōrn Cycles, https://www.bjorncycles.com, a maker of carbon-fibre ("composite") bicycle components.** It makes saddles, seatposts, handlebars and small parts. It ships from Slovenia (EU), prices in USD, contact info@bjorncycles.com, Instagram @bjorncycles, Facebook /bjorncycles. It has a dealers page; a US dealer is Fair Wheel Bikes (fairwheelbikes.com), which gave the specs below. The EU shop link (bjorncycles.eu) did not resolve for me.
- It is NOT a Brompton specialist and mentions no Brompton products. Brompton relevance is therefore by compatibility only (saddle rails, bottle cage). No Singapore dealer found. Owner buys by import (Slovenia/US) or a used ad.
- Not the same: "Bjorn Bikes" (Canadian recycled-steel bikes, sold at bike-components.de) is a different brand. I used the carbon-parts Bjorn Cycles because it is the only one that sells parts.
- Ruled out for this build: the Glagol seatpost (carbon posts excluded because of the child seat; also Brompton's telescopic post is not a standard round post), the Rool drop handlebar (drop bar, not a Brompton bar), the Lapka computer mount (a stem mount, not tested for Brompton).


### Saddles (the only part that realistically fits a Brompton)


All Bjorn saddles use oversized oval 7 x 9 mm carbon rails (seller claim, Fair Wheel Bikes page). Brompton's stock Penta-Clip clamps 7 mm round rails, so you need an adapter clamp (see below). Reference: Brompton stock saddle 378 g; Brompton Superlight Carbon saddle 205 g (retailer figure).

### Notes

- Bjorn prices are the US dealer's (Fair Wheel Bikes) in USD at 1.30; the maker's own USD prices could not be read (shop pages 404 or dead). Import duty/GST and shipping to Singapore are not included; free shipping above US$499 per bjorncycles.com.
- No Singapore, Carousell SG or Shopee SG Bjorn listing found.
- Cheaper way to the same weight goal: Brompton Superlight Carbon saddle 205 g (see round 1), but Sedlo saves about 115 g more.
